# KBR Live: full test checklist

Every feature and function the site offers, one line per testable behaviour. Built from the
code (every route, page, button, API endpoint, background job, setting, role, and stream
server (engine) vs browser-player station), then checked by an independent verifier until it
found nothing missing.

Row format: `- [ ] <id> <behaviour> | modes | covered: <test file or NOT COVERED>`.
Modes: **engine** = station played by the stream server (Liquidsoap), **browser** = station
played by the in-browser player, **both**, or n.a.

Every action row is also a speed row: the result must show (on screen and on air) within
the limits in section S. A row passes only when it does the right thing **and** is fast.

## S. Speed limits (apply to every action row)

- [x] S-1 Button press to visible change on screen (optimistic or confirmed): under 150 ms | both | covered: e2e/control-room-actions.mjs::speed "... within 150 ms (S-1)"; e2e/control-room-actions.mjs::actions "placeholder row shows at once (S-1)"
- [ ] S-2 Go next / Fade / Soft fade / Play now: next item audible in the control room within 0.5 s (browser) or 1.5 s (engine, incl. stream delay) | both | covered: partial (e2e/audio-engine.mjs browser only)
- [x] S-3 Every operator API call (skip, fire-now, insert, loop, mix-over, move, remove, reorder) answers in under 300 ms server time, 1 s round trip from the US East Coast | both | covered: tests/test_speed_operator_actions.py::test_operator_calls_answer_within_300_ms (server time, test DB); live: backend/loadtests/operator_speed.py
- [ ] S-4 Queue, now-playing and library search reads: under 300 ms server time; library search results on screen under 500 ms | n.a. | covered: NOT COVERED
- [x] S-5 Every admin page: first content under 1.5 s, interactive under 2.5 s on a warm load (desktop and iPhone) | n.a. | covered: frontend/e2e/admin-flows.mjs::S-5 desktop /admin/dashboard loads fast (+93 more)
- [x] S-6 Saves (schedule, block, playlist, station settings, program block, spot schedule): confirmed under 1 s; on-air effect on time | both | covered: frontend/e2e/admin-flows.mjs::every save step (timedSave, 1 s limit: stations, users, schedules, blocks, playlists, program blocks, spot schedules, rules ...); BUG spot schedule for all stations re-plans every station inside the save (F-SPOTSCHED-1 dayparts: 1.9-6.5 s with 80+ stations); FIXED: all-station spot save refreshes queues after the commit, tests/test_spot_save_speed_and_end_dates.py (scheduling thread, PR 169)
- [x] S-7 Listen (control room or public): sound within 1.5 s of the press; stream mode stays about 1 s behind live | both | covered: frontend/e2e/listen-page.mjs::basics "Listen Live heard within 1.5 s of the press (S-7)" (desktop 143 ms, iPhone 60 ms) + ::stream "stream heard within 1.5 s of Listen (S-7)" (133 ms) + "stays about 1 s behind live (S-7)" (0.51 s) + "fall-back heard within 1.5 s (S-7)" (90 ms) (public Listen on the fake backend; control room: control-room-actions.mjs)
- [x] S-8 Now-playing on every open screen updates within 1 s of a real change on air | both | covered: e2e/listen-page.mjs::basics "now playing updates within 1 s of a change on air (S-8)" + e2e/control-room-actions.mjs::transport "a change on air shows in the header within 1 s (S-8)"
- [ ] S-9 Hard timed item (time check, hard block, hard spot) heard within 1 s of its time | both | covered: harness (engine), pw10 scripts (browser, not in repo)
- [ ] S-11 iPhone Safari, server-stream station: Listen (control room and public Listen page) to sound within 2 s; never silent for 10 s or more (Shmuel, 2026-11-16 9:54 PM: about 10 s, then over 2 minutes of nothing) Real iPhone, Shmuel 2026-11-17 8:15 PM ET on KBR Jerusalem: "almost instant", and it keeps playing after the phone is locked. Still open: a clean hour of listening, lock-screen play/pause buttons, the control room page. Measured by Shmuel 8:32 PM ET: 2 seconds to first sound on KBR Jerusalem (the target). | engine | covered: partial, real WebKit (WebKitGTK), public Listen page only: frontend/e2e/webkit/listener-webkit.py::stream "keeps playing for 10 s, no stall (S-11)" (MSE and no-MSE paths pass) and "stream heard within 2 s (S-11)" (MSE path 0.2 s; no-MSE plain <audio> fallback 2.1 s: BUG xfail, GStreamer); the iPhone's HLS branch cannot run in WebKitGTK; control room not run in WebKit; real iPhone still not covered
- [x] S-10 Upload: progress shown at once; item playable in the library within 10 s of upload end for a 5 MB file | n.a. | covered: frontend/e2e/admin-flows.mjs::F-UPLOAD-2/4 + S-10 desktop: type, category, format, artist, album; Upload All shows progress at once; 5 MB... (+1 more)

## L. Look at the screen (applies to every row that uses a page)

Shmuel, 2026-11-17 11:10 PM ET: moving a song worked, but the screen showed a warning from another
station's rule, and the test had only asked whether the move succeeded. A row passes only when the
action worked **and** the screen, console and network showed nothing a person would call wrong.

- [x] L-1 Every browser test fails on an unexpected warning or error toast, alert, error page, console error, uncaught error, unhandled rejection, failed request or 4xx/5xx answer, even when all its own checks pass; what a test provokes on purpose is listed in it with a reason | n.a. | covered: frontend/e2e/lib/guard.mjs (guardPage, guardBrowser, guardReport) wired into every browser from e2e/lib/fake-station.mjs, judged in checker().done(); GUARD=report prints only
- [ ] L-2 Every existing browser test is clean under the guard or lists what it expects (control-room-song-actions done; the rest being worked through) | n.a. | covered: partial
- [x] L-3 Every staff page (36 routes) opens on each of the three stations with nothing wrong shown | both | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs "open" steps (local stack, e2e/walkthrough-seed.py)
- [x] L-4 Every control on every staff page (buttons, tabs, selects, checkboxes; destructive ones listed as not pressed) is pressed on each station: nothing wrong shown after the press, and it settles within the speed limit | both | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs "press ..." steps, a screenshot per press
- [x] L-5 Dragging a queue row with the mouse: the row moves, the order survives a reload, no warning or error shows | both | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs::dragInQueue (each station)
- [ ] L-6 Every control is fully visible and can be pressed at 1366x900: not off the side, not cut off by its box, not under another control (PC, 2026-11-18: Loop half under the Song button; queue-row Song / Move up / Move down never pressable with a mouse) | both | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs::unreachable + "could not be pressed" (FAILS on main: queue-row Song, Move up, Move down lie under something else)
- [ ] L-7 The same at phone size (390x844), and no page scrolls sideways | both | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs "look at phone 390x844" (first full run in progress)
- [x] L-8 A refused action always says so on the screen (Users: Disable on your own account was refused with nothing shown) | n.a. | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs /admin/users "press Disable" (alert "You cannot disable your own account")
- [x] L-9 Spot Schedule, Schedule Check, Program Blocks and Program Clock open on the station last worked on in this browser, not the first in the list; choosing a station there moves it for the others | n.a. | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs "opens on another station than the one last worked on" (each station)
- [x] L-10 A test leaves every station's modes (HS, Spots mode, Loop, automation settings) as it found them: every switch it presses is pressed back, and the run ends by comparing each station's settings with the start (Spots mode was left off on a live station by a test script, 2026-11-18) | n.a. | covered: frontend/e2e/walkthrough.mjs "the walk left this station changed"
- [ ] L-11 Every screenshot of a walk is looked at with fresh eyes for what a script cannot name (overlapping text, half-hidden labels, odd wording, a stale value); findings listed with the screenshot | n.a. | covered: by hand after each walk (index.html in the shots folder)
- [ ] L-12 A new release goes out while a page is open: opening every panel and tab that loads its code late still works (PC, 2026-11-18: "Failed to load module script ... MIME type text/html", databank stuck on Loading) | n.a. | covered: NOT COVERED
- [ ] L-13 Listen switched on, off and on again never stays on "Starting..." | both | covered: NOT COVERED (seen live by the PC 2026-11-18)
- [ ] L-14 The guard tells noise from failure: a cancelled song pre-load or version check is not a finding, a failed or refused request is | n.a. | covered: partial (net::ERR_ABORTED allowed by default; live noise list from the PC not yet encoded)
- [ ] L-15 Queue rows after midnight: the day name under the time does not run into the next row's time | both | covered: NOT COVERED (seen in the walk's screenshots 2026-11-18)

# Backend test checklist (exhaustive, from code)

Generated 2026-11-17 from backend/app (read-only). All API paths are under `/api/v1` unless noted. Guard in [brackets]; PUBLIC = no auth dependency. Coverage = path/function grep of backend/tests/ ("covered" means a test touches it, not that every branch is asserted). Row prefix ↳ = a behaviour/branch of the endpoint above it.

## 2. Background jobs and periodic behaviour

### 2a. Scheduler loop (`services/scheduler_engine.py`, every 3 s, leader-only)
- [x] B-JOB-1 Scheduler takes a 15 s DB lease; only the leader process advances stations; second process stays idle | modes: both | covered: tests/test_scheduler_lease.py
- [x] B-JOB-2 Lease handover when leader dies: follower takes over within ~15 s, no double advance | modes: both | covered: tests/test_scheduler_lease.py
- [x] B-JOB-3 Each pass uses its own session and ends its transaction (no "idle in transaction" blocking deploy migrations) | modes: both | covered: tests/test_scheduler_jobs.py::test_each_pass_ends_its_transaction
- [x] B-JOB-4 One station's exception rolls back, raises critical "Station check failed" alert, other stations still advance in the same pass | modes: both | covered: tests/test_scheduler_jobs.py::test_one_station_failing_does_not_stop_the_others
- [x] B-JOB-5 Browser station: `_advance_queue` -> `_check_advance` advances when duration elapsed (minus crossfade) | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_queue_advance.py
- [x] B-JOB-6 Browser: precise timer armed at cross_start (default 3 s before end; short/timed items 0.3 s) so server switch = client crossfade start | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_queue_advance.py, tests/test_song_change_gaps.py
- [x] B-JOB-7 Browser: poll does not advance before the end; precise timer does not advance too early | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_queue_advance.py
- [x] B-JOB-8 Browser: two concurrent advances (timer + poll + Next) promote only one entry (advance_lock) | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_queue_advance.py
- [x] B-JOB-9 Browser: duplicate "playing" rows cleaned to one (most recent) | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_scheduler_jobs.py::test_duplicate_playing_rows_are_cleaned_to_the_newest
- [x] B-JOB-10 Browser: playing entry with no started_at gets started_at=now | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_scheduler_jobs.py::test_playing_entry_without_start_time_gets_now
- [x] B-JOB-11 Browser: `_arm_timed_start` arms an exact timer for a hard timed item due within 10 s (top-of-hour time check starts on the second, not up to 3 s late) | modes: browser | covered: tests/test_timed_start_on_time.py

## V1. Added by verifier round 1

### A. Playout engine (infra/streaming/radio.liq, setup.sh, nginx, systemd, icecast.xml)

- [x] V1-1 Engine starts while the backend is down: /hello fails (logged), engine still starts and goes to the emergency lane; when the backend comes back the next /next works without a restart and the backend never got its hello (stale cued/timed state kept) | modes: engine | covered: harness engine_starts_backend_down (FIXED 2026-11-17: the hello was sent once; while the backend never got it, a time check handed over before the restart never aired. radio.liq now retries the hello every 5 s until it answers 200; the time check aired +1.3 s) | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:36-41
- [ ] V1-2 /next carries `given=` (last 5 handed entry ids, newest first); after a /flush the pre-loaded id is removed from `given` unless it already aired, so the backend can hand it out again | modes: engine | covered: NOT COVERED (backend side only, tests/test_playout_engine.py) | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:48-66, 229-236
- [ ] V1-3 request.dynamic retry: backend 204/error -> engine retries every 2 s (retry_delay), a track that takes >120 s to resolve/download is abandoned (timeout=120, PR 142) and the next is asked for; prefetch=1 means exactly one track pre-loaded | modes: engine | covered: NOT COVERED | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:68
- [x] V1-4d Every item gets a real loudness: an item analysed only from a few ranges of its file (no earlier full analysis) has its loudness measured from three 15 s pieces (20/50/80 %), within 1 dB of a full measurement; such items analysed before this (stored -18 LUFS / 0 dB with no measurement) are analysed again; output limiter at -2 dB so MP3 true peaks stay under 0 dBFS (Jerusalem 2026-11-17: items from -13.5 to -19.3 LUFS, true peaks to -0.2 dBFS) | modes: both | covered: tests/test_song_change_gaps.py::test_loudness_sampled_when_none_was_measured, ::test_unmeasured_loudness_is_analysed_again, ::test_combined_loudness_is_a_power_average + test_engine_infra_files.py::test_levels_and_output_processing (FIXED 2026-11-17) | src: backend/app/services/tail_analysis.py, backend/app/models/asset.py (has_real_loudness)
- [x] V1-4e Every item reaches the engine with its level: an item not yet analysed is analysed before /next hands it over (at most 6 s, then it goes without), and the next 3 items and timed items due within 15 min are analysed ahead by the tick; a sampled loudness louder than -5 LUFS is not believed (the whole file is measured instead); turning down goes to -20 dB (Jerusalem 2026-11-17 20:00Z: liq_amplify missing on every item analysed late, -19.9 to -10.6 LUFS on air) | modes: engine | covered: tests/test_engine_backend.py::test_item_gets_its_level_before_it_is_handed_over, ::test_upcoming_items_are_analysed_ahead; tests/test_song_change_gaps.py::test_unbelievable_sampled_loudness_falls_back_to_the_whole_file, ::test_bad_sampled_reading_is_analysed_again_and_loud_items_turn_down_further (FIXED 2026-11-17) | src: backend/app/services/playout_engine.py (ensure_level, _analyse_ahead)
- [x] V1-7b Output limiter holds true peaks: a full-scale master given +6 dB leaves the engine at no more than -3 dBFS sample peak and under -2 dBFS true peak in the MP3 stream, not crushed (still about -11 dBFS RMS), stream titles kept (FFmpeg alimiter with look-ahead; Liquidsoap limit() let -0.6 dBFS true peaks through) | modes: engine | covered: harness output_limiter (-4.3 dBFS sample, -4.2 dBFS true peak) + tests/test_engine_infra_files.py::test_levels_and_output_processing (FIXED 2026-11-17)
- [x] B-BLOCK-ANN A block's earlier items (its announcement) are left out when its programme (last recording) has nothing to play; the preview and Schedule Check say why | modes: both | covered: tests/test_program_blocks.py::test_an_announcement_is_left_out_when_its_programme_is_missing
- [x] V1-4c Engine alerts clear themselves: an open "Playout engine is on its emergency playlist" alert resolves when the engine plays a queue entry again; an open "<station>: Stream server and site disagree" alert resolves when the engine and the site agree (also on the first check after a restart); another station's alert stays open | modes: engine | covered: tests/test_engine_backend.py::test_emergency_alert_clears_itself_on_a_known_start + tests/test_air_watch.py::test_drift_alert_clears_itself_once_in_step
- [x] V1-4 Per-track level: `liq_amplify` (replay_gain_db from analysis) applied on the main lane and on mix-over; items with gain 0/no analysis play at unity | modes: engine | covered: partial: harness track_level (main lane: the same file with replay_gain_db -6 played 6.0 dB below the unity item; in a chain it played 12-18 dB below because every amplify read liq_amplify, FIXED 2026-11-17) + song_with_level_muted_under_time_check + tests/test_engine_infra_files.py::test_levels_and_output_processing. + harness timed_and_mix_over_levels (a -6 dB file plays at -30.5 dBFS on the timed lane and on mix-over, unity at -24.5). Note: a mix-over item keeps playing at its level under a hard time check (the overlay is added after the master gain) | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:70, 162; backend/app/services/playout_engine.py:186-189, 225-227
- [x] V1-5 Cue points sent to the engine: `liq_cue_in`/`liq_cue_out` from analysis are honoured (no lead-in silence, ends at cue_out); a paused shiur (resume@) starts at max(cue_in, resume offset) on the engine | modes: engine | covered: harness resume_position (2026-11-17: a shiur whose pitch rises with time, paused at 41.8 s for the time check, came back with resume@38 and the engine played from 39.4 s 1.5 s after the resume, i.e. from the offset); harness cue_points (cue_in 10 s / cue_out 40 s: heard from 10.8 s at 1.5 s in, next item 27.1 s after the start) | src: backend/app/services/playout_engine.py:208-225
- [x] V1-6 Output processing: compressor (-14 dB, 3:1) and limiter (-1 dB) on the whole output; no clipping on loud masters, quiet items not pumped | modes: engine | covered: harness output_limiter (true-peak limiter; stream peak measured), track_level | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:182-183
- [x] V1-7 Icecast output is 320 kbps / 44.1 kHz stereo MP3 with station name from KBR_STATION_NAME, genre and url set; listeners see the right stream title | modes: engine | covered: harness icecast_output (Icecast status: audio/mpeg 320, name "Kol B'ramah", genre, url; ffprobe of the stream a listener gets: mp3 44100 Hz 2 ch 320000; stream title "<artist> - <title>" of the item on air) | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:303-315
- [x] V1-8 Timed take-over arming window: the timed gate opens for only 1.5 s after the fade; a timed item whose file is not resolved by then (slow download, >45 s lead not enough) must still air (not wait for the next group or play late into a song) | modes: engine | covered: harness slow_timed_file (FIXED 2026-11-17: a time check whose file took 23 s to arrive muted the music at its time, 16 s of silence, and never aired (the timed queue's 20 s download limit dropped it). Now the take-over waits for the item to be ready while the music plays on, and the timed queue allows 90 s per download: aired +4.3 s, no silence) | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:128-147
- [x] V1-9 Timed group stays on air until every item of the group has played (timed_open while lane=timed and queue not empty), then music returns on the NEXT song, the interrupted song is not reported again | modes: engine | covered: harness three_item_timed_group (on the second, in order, each once, cut song not again, no silence) | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:114-118, 209-222
- [x] V1-10 Two timed groups less than 0.5 s apart are treated as one take-over (abs(at - timed_group_at) > 0.5); two groups 1 s apart both take over in order | modes: engine | covered: harness two_groups_0.3s_apart, two_groups_1s_apart | src: infra/streaming/radio.liq:150-155
- [x] V1-10b A hard timed group due while another (short) timed item is on air does not cut it: it follows it. The backend moves the later group to the end of the earlier one ahead of time (services/timed_groups.py: browser check 90 s ahead, engine tick before hand-over) and hands it to the engine at the first group's time so it plays inside the same take-over; not behind a shiur (long-item rule) or silence, at most 2 min | modes: both | covered: tests/test_timed_groups_follow.py (7 tests), harness two_groups_1s_apart (fixed: the time check was cut after 1 s by every player) | src: services/timed_groups.py; playout_engine._hand_over_timed; queue._check_advance_locked

### Areas checked

Every endpoint decorator in backend/app/api/v1/*.py (317, extracted by script) was matched against
the checklist by method and path: all have a row (four trailing-slash variants match the
slash-less rows). The rows above are therefore branches, background behaviour, engine/infra and
cross-cutting gaps, not missing endpoints.

- api/v1 routers: all 48 files scanned for raise/HTTPException/status branches and guards. Nothing further missing beyond the rows above in: activity, ai_emails, alerts, analytics, asset_types, categories, channels, copy_review, icecast, listeners, mixer, rds, reviews, rules, scheduler, site_release, sponsor_portal, sponsors, spot_schedules, streams, users (self-protection rows already exist as B-USERS-6/8).
- services: scheduler_engine, playout_engine, queue_replenish (main flow), scheduling, long_items, live_show_service (pause/resume), station_service, shabbos_service, weather_spot_service (zones), alert_service/spot_schedule/program_block constants checked against B-JOB rows. Constants for spot planning (7-day horizon, 2 min safety margin, slots 15/30/45) and program blocks (48 h) are already covered.
- main.py: lifespan steps, /health, /api/v1/init, fallback middleware, migrations: covered except V1-111..113.
- config.py: every setting matched; only gaps are V1-37, V1-39, V1-106 (S3_*, FFMPEG_PATH, LOG_LEVEL, FRONTEND_URL are low-risk and folded into existing B-SET rows).
- infra/streaming: radio.liq, setup.sh, nginx-stream.conf, kbr-engine@.service, icecast.xml, kbr-engine.env.example: the checklist had almost nothing (only B-MODE-23..26); see section A.
- frontend: App.tsx routes all have a section; localStorage/sessionStorage keys, keyboard handlers, useAudioEngine, useNowPlayingWS, liveStream, Listen, appUpdate scanned. Control-room panels (TransportBar, QueuePanel, LibraryPanel, HotButtons, PflDeck, SidePanels) are well covered by F-CR rows; nothing further missing there.
- Not swept line by line (time): enhance_service, audio_convert_service, bpm/musicbrainz, mixer_service, reviews internals, CRM rankings, analytics SQL, activity_log internals, bug_report_service internals. Endpoint-level rows for these already exist.

# Third pass: things nobody had thought to test (comb-through 2026-11-18)

Seeds were the surprises of 2026-11-17: a song chopped when the previous one ended seconds before the hour, music under a live host on the stream server, an engine whose /status died before its first track, a test that fails in the first ten minutes of the UTC day, a login that expired in the middle of a long script, two engines on one core, a sponsorship block with more spots than room. Each row says where it can be tested: H = stream-server harness, T = automated test, J = live on KBR Jerusalem by the PC, D = on the droplet, P = real phone.

## V3a. The clock
- [ ] V3-1 The whole backend test suite passes whatever the time: run it with the clock set to 00:03 UTC, 23:57 UTC, hh:59:55, 05:03 UTC (New York midnight), a Friday night and a Saturday (Shabbos blackout in force), 29 February, 31 December 23:59, and both daylight-saving change days | modes: n.a. | covered: partial: full suite (1,246 tests, faketime, 2026-11-18) passes at 00:03 UTC, 23:52 UTC and 31 Dec 23:55; 12 failures at a Saturday 15:00 UTC, 04:56 UTC and 2027-03-14 06:50 UTC were all faults of the tests (a "60 days ago" that passes the tested dates from January 2027, a month-based summer-time guess, a Shabbos window in progress, a speed limit missed on a busy machine) and are fixed; no product fault. Still to run: hh:59:55, a Friday night, 29 February, the autumn change day
- [ ] V3-2 A backend that stays up across midnight (UTC and station time): "today" values roll over without a restart: listener counts, song-request daily caps, the day's weather spot, day log, activity "today" counts | modes: both | covered: NOT COVERED (T)
- [ ] V3-3 A song that ends 0, 1, 2, ... 9 s before a hard item, each second: never a song started and cut; quiet up to 3 s, else the hard item starts early; the hard item airs whole and once | modes: engine | covered: partial (harness song_ends_before_hour 1.5 s and 6 s pass after PR 232; the other seconds not swept yet) (H sweep)
- [ ] V3-4 The same in the browser player (song ends 0 to 9 s before a hard item) | modes: browser | covered: NOT COVERED (T e2e/audio-engine.mjs)
- [x] V3-5 Two hard items due on the same second from different sources (time check, a program block, a sponsorship block): one order, each once, none dropped | modes: both | covered: harness two_sources_same_second (9 of 9 checks, 2026-11-18, engine); browser player NOT COVERED
- [x] V3-6 A hard group still playing when the next hard item is due (group longer than the gap, e.g. :00 block of 16 min and a :15 item): the later one waits or cuts by rule, never overlaps, never lost | modes: both | covered: harness hard_group_overruns_next_hard (5 of 5, 2026-11-18: the later item waits and airs once the group ends); browser player NOT COVERED
- [ ] V3-7 A hard item due while emergency music is on air, and with an empty queue: it still airs on its second | modes: engine | covered: NOT COVERED (H)
- [ ] V3-8 The hour's time-check recording is missing or unreadable: something sensible airs (fallback voice or nothing), no silence, no stale hour, an alert | modes: both | covered: NOT COVERED (T, H)
- [ ] V3-9 The day's weather recording arrives late or not at all: yesterday's is never aired as today's | modes: both | covered: NOT COVERED (T)
- [ ] V3-10 Backend and stream-server clocks 2 s apart, and a 1 s clock step on either during play: timed items within 2 s, nothing twice | modes: engine | covered: NOT COVERED (H with faketime on the engine)
- [ ] V3-11 Israel and US daylight saving change on different dates: in those weeks KBR Jerusalem's time check says the right hour and Friday times are right | modes: both | covered: NOT COVERED (T)
- [ ] V3-12 A program block or schedule block that crosses midnight, and one on a weekday boundary (days_of_week, 0 = Monday): airs on the right day, once | modes: both | covered: NOT COVERED (T)
